Solved counts are out of the true and false cases, declined correctly out of the contingent ones (a contingent case is never solved: the right response there is no verdict); —: no such case.

Timing

Solved cases: 5.92 s in total, median 0.0652 s per case, over 88 solved case(s) with a time. Times are wall-clock seconds per query as the runner measured them (each query in its own forked process; setup and warm-up not timed); totals and medians are over the solved cases only, so a timeout, error or no verdict never counts as a fast answer. They are shown next to the counts, never combined with them.

Measured on machine pi5 · indicative: it has some other load: times are a fair guide, large differences between its runs mean something, small ones may be noise. Raspberry Pi 5, 4 cores. Benchmark runs go one at a time, in a low-priority systemd unit (CPUWeight=20, Nice=10); since 2026-10-17, each eval is pinned to core 3 (earlier runs were not pinned). The Pi also serves websites, and other work may share core 3 unless it is reserved for the runs (pi5-deploy INSTALL.md, step 15). Clock frequency and temperature are not controlled.

measured setup time: 0.137 s
